## Existing Guards and Scope Limits

`🧰️framework/🛍️products/🦑️repo/🔨️modules/📚️library/🔍️discovery/🕸️runtime/🟦️.ts:34` implements runtimeFixturePathV1 and exempts direct-child modules. Its production traversal rejects fixture-owned sources/resources; verification reads retained compiler inputs, proc-macro inputs, build-producer filesystem inputs and physical source ownership. Genuine module named 🧫️examples under 🔨️modules must remain exempt; name matching alone cannot decide collection ownership. No nonempty direct-child collection module appeared in this file inventory.

Current root script reads job law fixtures at lines 5444, 5580, 5644 and ownership fixture at 7413 from outside tests; these are verification consumers, not proven runtime inputs. Framework OS playbook/workflow test include_str! paths currently point to sibling fixtures, e.g. playbook/🧪️tests/📖️playbook/🦀️.rs:6 and workflow/🧪️tests/🔁️workflow/🦀️.rs:405. Repo library Nx project inputs also name outside-test fixtures at lines 47, 620, 636. These must follow moves.

The architectural traversal currently permits/skips fixtures outside tests (`🚪️io/🏛️architecture/🟦️.ts:122,192,198`), so it does not enforce strict placement. A dedicated placement guard is needed.

No runtime or release-package fixture edge is proven from this audit alone. Existing runtime verifier is the appropriate authority, and production feature selection plus compiler observation are necessary before making a zero-edge assertion. Bounded text inspection of .github/workflows yielded no fixture strings; this does not prove packaging closure purity.

## Supplemental Directory Inventory

A complete os.walk directory pass with .git, node_modules, target, .nx, .🧬semio, dist, build, .venv excluded found 2120 fixture roots, of which 2019 are outside recognized test ancestors. This includes empty/ignored collections missed by rg and uses only fixture names (not example names), so it is a distinct coverage set and not directly comparable to the 2078 file-bearing combined set.
